{
  "instance_id": "bird001",
  "db": "california_schools",
  "question": "For locally funded schools whose K-12 minus ages-5-17 enrollment difference exceeds the average such difference among locally funded schools, list each school's directory-listed name and DOC type.",
  "evidence": ""
}

SQL diff

--- before.sql
+++ after.sql
@@ -0,0 +1,14 @@
+WITH locally_funded AS (
+    SELECT f.CDSCode,
+           f."Enrollment (K-12)" - f."Enrollment (Ages 5-17)" AS enrollment_difference
+    FROM frpm AS f
+    WHERE f."Charter Funding Type" = 'Locally funded'
+)
+SELECT s.School, s.DOCType
+FROM locally_funded AS lf
+JOIN schools AS s ON s.CDSCode = lf.CDSCode
+WHERE lf.enrollment_difference > (
+    SELECT AVG(enrollment_difference)
+    FROM locally_funded
+)
+LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH locally_funded AS (
    SELECT f.CDSCode,
           f."Enrollment (K-12)" - f."Enrollment (Ages 5-17)" AS enrollment_difference
    FROM frpm AS f
    WHERE f."Charter Funding Type" = 'Locally funded'
)
SELECT s.School, s.DOCType
FROM locally_funded AS lf
JOIN schools AS s ON s.CDSCode = lf.CDSCode
WHERE lf.enrollment_difference > (
    SELECT AVG(enrollment_difference)
    FROM locally_funded
)
LIMIT 1000

A card is identified by its Japanese name together with its English card type - printings that share both count as one card, and if either differs they should be listed as separate cards. Final SQL / answer

WITH japanese_printings AS (
    SELECT f.name AS japanese_name,
           c.type AS english_type,
           c.uuid,
           MAX(c.watermark IS 'boros') AS has_boros_watermark
    FROM main.cards AS c
    JOIN main.foreign_data AS f ON f.uuid = c.uuid
    WHERE f.language = 'Japanese'
    GROUP BY f.name, c.type, c.uuid
)
SELECT p.japanese_name,
       p.english_type,
       COUNT(r.id) AS total_rulings
FROM japanese_printings AS p
LEFT JOIN main.rulings AS r ON r.uuid = p.uuid
GROUP BY p.japanese_name, p.english_type
HAVING MAX(p.has_boros_watermark) = 1
ORDER BY p.japanese_name, p.english_type
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    j.name AS japanese_name,
    c.type AS english_card_type,
    COUNT(DISTINCT r.id) AS total_rulings
FROM foreign_data AS j
JOIN cards AS c ON c.uuid = j.uuid
LEFT JOIN rulings AS r ON r.uuid = c.uuid
WHERE j.language = 'Japanese'
GROUP BY j.name, c.type
HAVING MAX(CASE WHEN c.watermark = 'boros' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 1
ORDER BY total_rulings DESC, j.name, c.type
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    100.0 * (
        COUNT(CASE WHEN strftime('%Y', b.Date) = '2010' THEN 1 END)
        - COUNT(CASE WHEN strftime('%Y', b.Date) = '2011' THEN 1 END)
    ) / NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0) AS difference_percentage_points
FROM badges AS b
WHERE b.Name = 'Student'
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    100.0 * (
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE strftime('%Y', Date) = '2010')
        - COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE strftime('%Y', Date) = '2011')
    ) / NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0) AS difference_percentage_points
FROM main.badges
WHERE Name = 'Student'
LIMIT 1

  "db": "codebase_community",
  "question": "I'm profiling how seasoned our Organizer badge holders in Germany are. Among the users who earned the Organizer badge and are based in Germany, how many had already earned 5 badges before they got the Organizer badge? Just the count, please. Note: \"before\" means strictly earlier - badges earned at the same moment as the Organizer badge don't count toward the 5, and the Organizer badge itself doesn't count as one of the 5.",
  "evidence": "Organizer badge: the badge whose name is Organizer."

Final SQL / answer

WITH organizer_awards AS (
    SELECT o.UserId, MIN(o.Date) AS organizer_date
    FROM badges AS o
    JOIN users AS u ON u.Id = o.UserId
    WHERE o.Name = 'Organizer'
      AND (
          u.Location IN ('Germany', 'Munich', 'Minga')
          OR u.Location LIKE '%, Germany'
      )
    GROUP BY o.UserId
),
qualifying_users AS (
    SELECT o.UserId
    FROM organizer_awards AS o
    JOIN badges AS b
      ON b.UserId = o.UserId
     AND b.Date < o.organizer_date
    GROUP BY o.UserId
    HAVING COUNT(*) = 5
)
SELECT COUNT(*) AS user_count
FROM qualifying_users
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH organizers AS (
    SELECT b.UserId, MIN(b.Date) AS organizer_date
    FROM badges AS b
    JOIN users AS u ON u.Id = b.UserId
    WHERE b.Name = 'Organizer'
      AND u.Location LIKE '%Germany%'
    GROUP BY b.UserId
),
qualified_users AS (
    SELECT o.UserId
    FROM organizers AS o
    JOIN badges AS prior ON prior.UserId = o.UserId
                        AND prior.Date < o.organizer_date
    GROUP BY o.UserId
    HAVING COUNT(*) >= 5
)
SELECT COUNT(*) AS user_count
FROM qualified_users
LIMIT 1000

SELECT u.Id
FROM main.users AS u
WHERE u.DisplayName = 'Michael'
  AND u.CreationDate IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY u.CreationDate DESC, u.Id ASC
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(*) AS transaction_count
FROM main.trans AS t
WHERE NULLIF(TRIM(t.k_symbol), '') IS NULL
  AND t.account_id IN (
      SELECT d.account_id
      FROM main.disp AS d
      JOIN main.client AS c ON c.client_id = d.client_id
      WHERE d.type = 'OWNER'
        AND c.gender = 'M'
        AND c.birth_date >= '1974-01-01'
        AND c.birth_date < '1977-01-01'
  )
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(*) AS transaction_count
FROM main.trans AS t
WHERE (t.k_symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(t.k_symbol) = '')
  AND t.account_id IN (
      SELECT d.account_id
      FROM main.disp AS d
      JOIN main.client AS c ON c.client_id = d.client_id
      WHERE d.type = 'OWNER'
        AND c.gender = 'M'
        AND c.birth_date >= '1974-01-01'
        AND c.birth_date < '1977-01-01'
  )
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH cardholders AS (
    SELECT
        cl.client_id,
        cl.gender,
        cl.birth_date,
        c.type AS card_type,
        CAST(strftime('%Y', c.issued) AS INTEGER)
            - CAST(strftime('%Y', cl.birth_date) AS INTEGER) AS age_at_card_issue,
        dp.account_id,
        cl.district_id
    FROM main.card AS c
    JOIN main.disp AS dp ON dp.disp_id = c.disp_id
    JOIN main.client AS cl ON cl.client_id = dp.client_id
    WHERE c.issued >= '1994-01-01' AND c.issued < '1995-01-01'
),
account_loans AS (
    SELECT
        l.account_id,
        COUNT(*) AS number_of_loans,
        SUM(l.amount) AS total_amount_borrowed
    FROM main.loan AS l
    GROUP BY l.account_id
)
SELECT
    ch.client_id,
    ch.gender,
    ch.birth_date,
    ch.card_type,
    ch.age_at_card_issue,
    COALESCE(al.number_of_loans, 0) AS number_of_loans,
    COALESCE(al.total_amount_borrowed, 0) AS total_amount_borrowed,
    CASE
        WHEN al.number_of_loans > 0 AND ch.age_at_card_issue < 30 THEN 'Young borrower'
        WHEN al.number_of_loans > 0 AND ch.age_at_card_issue >= 30 THEN 'Mature borrower'
        ELSE 'Non-borrower'
    END AS borrower_classification,
    dt.A2 AS district_name,
    dt.A11 AS district_average_salary,
    RANK() OVER (
        PARTITION BY ch.gender
        ORDER BY ch.age_at_card_issue DESC
    ) AS age_rank_within_gender
FROM cardholders AS ch
LEFT JOIN account_loans AS al ON al.account_id = ch.account_id
LEFT JOIN main.district AS dt ON dt.district_id = ch.district_id
ORDER BY ch.client_id
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH account_loans AS (
    SELECT account_id, COUNT(*) AS loan_count, SUM(amount) AS total_amount_borrowed
    FROM loan
    GROUP BY account_id
),
profiles AS (
    SELECT c.client_id, c.gender, c.birth_date, k.type AS card_type,
           CAST(strftime('%Y', k.issued) AS INTEGER)
               - CAST(strftime('%Y', c.birth_date) AS INTEGER) AS age_at_issue,
           COALESCE(l.loan_count, 0) AS loan_count,
           COALESCE(l.total_amount_borrowed, 0) AS total_amount_borrowed,
           d.A2 AS district_name, d.A11 AS district_average_salary
    FROM card AS k
    JOIN disp AS p ON p.disp_id = k.disp_id
    JOIN client AS c ON c.client_id = p.client_id
    JOIN district AS d ON d.district_id = c.district_id
    LEFT JOIN account_loans AS l ON l.account_id = p.account_id
    WHERE k.issued >= '1994-01-01' AND k.issued < '1995-01-01'
)
SELECT client_id, gender, birth_date, card_type, age_at_issue,
       loan_count, total_amount_borrowed,
       CASE
           WHEN loan_count > 0 AND age_at_issue < 30 THEN 'Young borrower'
           WHEN loan_count > 0 AND age_at_issue >= 30 THEN 'Mature borrower'
           ELSE 'Non-borrower'
       END AS borrower_classification,
       district_name, district_average_salary,
       RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY gender ORDER BY age_at_issue DESC) AS age_rank_within_gender
FROM profiles
ORDER BY client_id
LIMIT -1

Final SQL / answer

WITH race_winner_times AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT r.year, r.raceId, res.milliseconds
    FROM races AS r
    JOIN results AS res ON res.raceId = r.raceId
    WHERE r.year < 1975
      AND res.position = 1
      AND res.milliseconds IS NOT NULL
)
SELECT
    year,
    ((SUM(milliseconds) + 5 * COUNT(*)) / (10 * COUNT(*))) / 100.0
        AS mean_winner_elapsed_seconds
FROM race_winner_times
GROUP BY year
ORDER BY year
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    r.year,
    ((SUM(w.milliseconds) + 5 * COUNT(*)) / (10 * COUNT(*))) / 100.0 AS mean_winner_seconds
FROM races AS r
JOIN results AS w ON w.raceId = r.raceId
WHERE r.year < 1975
  AND w.position = 1
  AND w.milliseconds IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY r.year
ORDER BY r.year
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    d.forename,
    d.surname,
    strftime(
        '%H:%M:%f',
        r.time,
        printf('+%.3f seconds', result.milliseconds / 1000.0)
    ) AS local_finish_time,
    d.url
FROM races AS r
JOIN results AS result ON result.raceId = r.raceId
JOIN drivers AS d ON d.driverId = result.driverId
WHERE r.year = 2017
  AND r.name = 'Azerbaijan Grand Prix'
  AND result.position = 1
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN d.forename = 'Peter' THEN d.driverId END) AS Peter,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN d.forename = 'John' THEN d.driverId END) AS John
FROM main.drivers AS d
JOIN main.results AS r ON r.driverId = d.driverId
WHERE r.position = 1
  AND d.forename IN ('Peter', 'John')
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT
    COUNT(DISTINCT d.driverId) FILTER (WHERE d.forename = 'Peter') AS Peter,
    COUNT(DISTINCT d.driverId) FILTER (WHERE d.forename = 'John') AS John
FROM results AS r
JOIN drivers AS d ON d.driverId = r.driverId
WHERE r.position = 1
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH member_counts AS (
    SELECT mb.link_to_major, COUNT(*) AS member_count
    FROM main.member AS mb
    GROUP BY mb.link_to_major
)
SELECT
    m.department,
    GROUP_CONCAT(m.major_name, ', ' ORDER BY m.major_name) AS majors_offered,
    GROUP_CONCAT(m.major_name, ', ' ORDER BY m.major_name)
        FILTER (WHERE mc.member_count > 0) AS majors_held_by_members,
    ROUND(AVG(COALESCE(mc.member_count, 0)), 2) AS avg_members_per_major
FROM main.major AS m
LEFT JOIN member_counts AS mc ON mc.link_to_major = m.major_id
GROUP BY m.department
HAVING COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE m.college = 'College of Humanities and Social Sciences') > 0
ORDER BY m.department
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH members_per_major AS (
    SELECT m.department, m.major_id, m.major_name, COUNT(r.member_id) AS member_count
    FROM main.major AS m
    LEFT JOIN main.member AS r ON r.link_to_major = m.major_id
    WHERE m.department IN (
        SELECT department
        FROM main.major
        WHERE college = 'College of Humanities and Social Sciences'
    )
    GROUP BY m.department, m.major_id, m.major_name
)
SELECT department,
       json_group_array(major_name ORDER BY major_name, major_id) AS majors_offered,
       json_group_array(major_name ORDER BY major_name, major_id)
           FILTER (WHERE member_count > 0) AS majors_held_by_members,
       ROUND(AVG(member_count), 2) AS average_members_per_major
FROM members_per_major
GROUP BY department
ORDER BY department
LIMIT 1000

WITH latest_expense AS (
    SELECT MAX(e.expense_date) AS expense_date
    FROM main.expense AS e
    JOIN main.member AS m ON m.member_id = e.link_to_member
    WHERE m.position = 'Vice President'
)
SELECT e.expense_date, SUM(e.cost) AS total_cost
FROM main.expense AS e
JOIN latest_expense AS latest ON latest.expense_date = e.expense_date
GROUP BY e.expense_date
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT e.expense_date, SUM(e.cost) AS total_cost
FROM main.expense AS e
WHERE e.expense_date = (
    SELECT MAX(vp.expense_date)
    FROM main.expense AS vp
    JOIN main.member AS m ON m.member_id = vp.link_to_member
    WHERE m.position = 'Vice President'
)
GROUP BY e.expense_date
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH event_budgets AS (
    SELECT b.link_to_event, SUM(b.amount) AS total_budgeted
    FROM budget AS b
    GROUP BY b.link_to_event
),
event_attendance AS (
    SELECT a.link_to_event, COUNT(a.link_to_member) AS members_attended
    FROM attendance AS a
    GROUP BY a.link_to_event
)
SELECT
    e.event_name,
    e.event_date,
    b.total_budgeted,
    COALESCE(a.members_attended, 0) AS members_attended
FROM event AS e
LEFT JOIN event_budgets AS b ON b.link_to_event = e.event_id
LEFT JOIN event_attendance AS a ON a.link_to_event = e.event_id
ORDER BY e.event_name ASC, e.event_date ASC
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH event_budgets AS (
    SELECT link_to_event, SUM(amount) AS total_budgeted
    FROM main.budget
    GROUP BY link_to_event
),
event_attendance AS (
    SELECT link_to_event, COUNT(link_to_member) AS members_attended
    FROM main.attendance
    GROUP BY link_to_event
)
SELECT
    e.event_name,
    e.event_date,
    COALESCE(b.total_budgeted, 0) AS total_budgeted,
    COALESCE(a.members_attended, 0) AS members_attended
FROM main.event AS e
LEFT JOIN event_budgets AS b ON b.link_to_event = e.event_id
LEFT JOIN event_attendance AS a ON a.link_to_event = e.event_id
ORDER BY e.event_name ASC, e.event_date ASC
LIMIT -1

Final SQL / answer

WITH RECURSIVE descriptions(description) AS (
    SELECT CASE
               WHEN e.expense_description GLOB 'Bakery - *'
               THEN substr(e.expense_description, length('Bakery - ') + 1)
               ELSE e.expense_description
           END
    FROM main.expense AS e
    JOIN main.member AS m ON m.member_id = e.link_to_member
    WHERE m.first_name = 'Sacha'
      AND m.last_name = 'Harrison'
),
items(item_name, remaining) AS (
    SELECT '', description || ','
    FROM descriptions
    UNION ALL
    SELECT trim(substr(remaining, 1, instr(remaining, ',') - 1)),
           substr(remaining, instr(remaining, ',') + 1)
    FROM items
    WHERE remaining <> ''
)
SELECT item_name, COUNT(*) AS purchase_count
FROM items
WHERE item_name <> ''
GROUP BY item_name
ORDER BY item_name
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH RECURSIVE items(expense_id, item_name, remaining) AS (
    SELECT e.expense_id, NULL, e.expense_description || ','
    FROM main.expense AS e
    JOIN main.member AS m ON m.member_id = e.link_to_member
    WHERE m.first_name = 'Sacha' AND m.last_name = 'Harrison'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT expense_id,
           trim(substr(remaining, 1, instr(remaining, ',') - 1)),
           substr(remaining, instr(remaining, ',') + 1)
    FROM items
    WHERE remaining <> ''
),
named_items AS (
    SELECT expense_id,
           CASE WHEN item_name GLOB 'Bakery - *'
                THEN substr(item_name, length('Bakery - ') + 1)
                ELSE item_name
           END AS item_name
    FROM items
    WHERE item_name <> ''
)
SELECT item_name, COUNT(DISTINCT expense_id) AS purchase_count
FROM named_items
GROUP BY item_name
ORDER BY purchase_count DESC, item_name
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH RECURSIVE period_expenses AS (
    SELECT e.link_to_member AS member_id,
           e.expense_description,
           e.cost
    FROM expense AS e
    WHERE e.expense_date >= '2019-10-01'
      AND e.expense_date < '2019-12-01'
),
split_items(member_id, item, remainder) AS (
    SELECT p.member_id, NULL, p.expense_description || ','
    FROM period_expenses AS p
    UNION ALL
    SELECT s.member_id,
           LOWER(TRIM(SUBSTR(s.remainder, 1, INSTR(s.remainder, ',') - 1))),
           SUBSTR(s.remainder, INSTR(s.remainder, ',') + 1)
    FROM split_items AS s
    WHERE s.remainder <> ''
),
item_counts AS (
    SELECT s.member_id, COUNT(DISTINCT s.item) AS kind_count
    FROM split_items AS s
    WHERE s.item <> ''
    GROUP BY s.member_id
),
spending AS (
    SELECT p.member_id, ROUND(SUM(p.cost), 2) AS total_spending
    FROM period_expenses AS p
    GROUP BY p.member_id
),
ranked_members AS (
    SELECT m.member_id,
           m.first_name || ' ' || m.last_name AS full_name,
           i.kind_count,
           s.total_spending,
           DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY i.kind_count DESC) AS kind_rank
    FROM member AS m
    JOIN item_counts AS i ON i.member_id = m.member_id
    JOIN spending AS s ON s.member_id = m.member_id
)
SELECT r.full_name, r.kind_count, r.total_spending
FROM ranked_members AS r
WHERE r.kind_rank = 1
ORDER BY r.full_name, r.member_id
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

WITH RECURSIVE period_expenses AS (
    SELECT e.link_to_member AS member_id, e.expense_description, e.cost
    FROM main.expense AS e
    WHERE e.expense_date >= '2019-10-01'
      AND e.expense_date < '2019-12-01'
),
items(member_id, item_name, remaining) AS (
    SELECT member_id, NULL, expense_description || ','
    FROM period_expenses
    UNION ALL
    SELECT member_id,
           LOWER(TRIM(SUBSTR(remaining, 1, INSTR(remaining, ',') - 1))),
           SUBSTR(remaining, INSTR(remaining, ',') + 1)
    FROM items
    WHERE remaining <> ''
),
kind_counts AS (
    SELECT member_id, COUNT(DISTINCT item_name) AS kind_count
    FROM items
    WHERE item_name <> ''
    GROUP BY member_id
),
spending AS (
    SELECT member_id, ROUND(SUM(cost), 2) AS total_spending_dollars
    FROM period_expenses
    GROUP BY member_id
)
SELECT m.first_name || ' ' || m.last_name AS full_name,
       k.kind_count,
       s.total_spending_dollars
FROM kind_counts AS k
JOIN main.member AS m ON m.member_id = k.member_id
JOIN spending AS s ON s.member_id = k.member_id
WHERE k.kind_count = (SELECT MAX(kind_count) FROM kind_counts)
ORDER BY full_name, m.member_id
LIMIT -1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(*) AS superhero_count
FROM superhero AS s
JOIN alignment AS a ON a.id = s.alignment_id
WHERE a.alignment NOT IN ('Good', 'Bad')
LIMIT 1000

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS publisher_count
FROM main.publisher AS p
JOIN main.superhero AS s ON s.publisher_id = p.id
WHERE s.height_cm > 200
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT COUNT(*) AS publisher_count
FROM main.publisher AS p
WHERE p.id IN (
    SELECT s.publisher_id
    FROM main.superhero AS s
    WHERE s.height_cm > 200
)
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH hero_power_counts AS (
    SELECT s.id, g.gender, COUNT(hp.power_id) AS power_count
    FROM main.superhero AS s
    JOIN main.gender AS g ON g.id = s.gender_id
    LEFT JOIN main.hero_power AS hp ON hp.hero_id = s.id
    WHERE g.gender IN ('Male', 'Female')
      AND INSTR(TRIM(s.full_name), ' ') > 0
      AND TRIM(s.full_name) NOT IN (
          'Anna Marie',
          'Diana of Themyscira',
          'Howard (Last name unrevealed)',
          'Krypto the Superdog',
          'Mr. Mxyzptlk',
          'Ra''s Al Ghul',
          'Weapon XI',
          'Yurrd the Unknown'
      )
      AND TRIM(s.full_name) NOT LIKE 'Cyberdyne Systems Series %'
    GROUP BY s.id, g.gender
)
SELECT ROUND(AVG(power_count) FILTER (WHERE gender = 'Male'), 2) AS male_average,
       ROUND(AVG(power_count) FILTER (WHERE gender = 'Female'), 2) AS female_average
FROM hero_power_counts
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH hero_counts AS (
    SELECT h.id, g.gender, COUNT(p.power_id) AS power_count
    FROM main.superhero AS h
    JOIN main.gender AS g ON g.id = h.gender_id
    LEFT JOIN main.hero_power AS p ON p.hero_id = h.id
    WHERE g.gender IN ('Male', 'Female')
      AND INSTR(TRIM(h.full_name), ' ') > 0
    GROUP BY h.id, g.gender
)
SELECT
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN gender = 'Male' THEN power_count END), 2) AS male_average,
    ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN gender = 'Female' THEN power_count END), 2) AS female_average
FROM hero_counts
LIMIT 1

+SELECT ROUND(100.0 * AVG(CASE WHEN a.alignment = 'Good' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 2) AS good_percentage
+FROM superhero AS s
+JOIN publisher AS p ON p.id = s.publisher_id
+LEFT JOIN alignment AS a ON a.id = s.alignment_id
+WHERE p.publisher_name = 'Marvel Comics'
+  AND TRIM(s.full_name) NOT IN ('', '-')

Final SQL / answer

Should single-name entries count as documented full names? The catalog stores both `T'Challa` and `Bob` in `full_name` without indicating which entries are complete civilian identities.

Final SQL / answer

SELECT ROUND(100.0 * AVG(CASE WHEN a.alignment = 'Good' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 2) AS good_percentage
FROM superhero AS s
JOIN publisher AS p ON p.id = s.publisher_id
LEFT JOIN alignment AS a ON a.id = s.alignment_id
WHERE p.publisher_name = 'Marvel Comics'
  AND TRIM(s.full_name) NOT IN ('', '-')
LIMIT 1

SELECT
    COUNT(CASE WHEN c.colour = 'No Colour' THEN 1 END)
    - COUNT(CASE WHEN c.colour = 'Blue' THEN 1 END) AS difference
FROM main.superhero AS s
JOIN main.colour AS c ON c.id = s.eye_colour_id
WHERE s.weight_kg IS NULL
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT ROUND(
    100.0 * COUNT(CASE WHEN p.SEX = 'F' THEN 1 END) / NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0),
    2
) AS female_percentage
FROM main.Patient AS p
WHERE DATE(p.Birthday) <= DATE('2026-10-09', '-56 years')
  AND EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM main.Laboratory AS l
      WHERE l.ID = p.ID
        AND l.PT >= 14
  )
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT ROUND(
    100.0 * COUNT(CASE WHEN p.SEX = 'F' THEN 1 END) / NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0),
    2
) AS female_percentage
FROM Patient AS p
WHERE DATE(p.Birthday) < DATE('2026-10-09', '-55 years')
  AND EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM Laboratory AS l
      WHERE l.ID = p.ID
        AND l.PT >= 14
  )
LIMIT 1;

Final SQL / answer

WITH RECURSIVE diagnosis_tokens(patient_id, diagnosis, remaining) AS (
    SELECT Patient.ID,
           '',
           CASE trim(Patient.Diagnosis, char(9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 32))
               WHEN 'SJS  RA' THEN 'SJS,RA'
               WHEN 'RA  SJS' THEN 'RA,SJS'
               ELSE replace(Patient.Diagnosis, char(29), ',')
           END || ','
    FROM Patient
    WHERE instr(Patient.Diagnosis, 'RA') > 0
      AND instr(Patient.Diagnosis, 'SJS') > 0

    UNION ALL

    SELECT patient_id,
           trim(substr(remaining, 1, instr(remaining, ',') - 1),
                char(9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 32)),
           substr(remaining, instr(remaining, ',') + 1)
    FROM diagnosis_tokens
    WHERE remaining <> ''
),
eligible_patients AS (
    SELECT patient_id
    FROM diagnosis_tokens
    WHERE diagnosis <> ''
      AND rtrim(diagnosis, '.') NOT LIKE '% susp'
    GROUP BY patient_id
    HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT diagnosis) = 2
       AND MIN(diagnosis) = 'RA'
       AND MAX(diagnosis) = 'SJS'
),
normal_platelet_patients AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT Laboratory.ID
    FROM Laboratory
    WHERE Laboratory.PLT > 100
      AND Laboratory.PLT < 400
)
SELECT COUNT(normal_platelet_patients.ID) AS patients_with_normal_platelets,
       COUNT(*) - COUNT(normal_platelet_patients.ID) AS patients_without_normal_platelets
FROM eligible_patients
LEFT JOIN normal_platelet_patients
    ON normal_platelet_patients.ID = eligible_patients.patient_id
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH platelet_history AS (
    SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM main.Laboratory AS l
        WHERE l.ID = p.ID
          AND l.PLT > 100
          AND l.PLT < 400
    ) AS had_normal_platelets
    FROM main.Patient AS p
    WHERE p.Diagnosis IN ('RA, SJS', 'RA,SJS', 'SJS, RA', 'SJS  RA')
)
SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE had_normal_platelets = 1) AS ever_normal_platelets,
       COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE had_normal_platelets = 0) AS never_normal_platelets
FROM platelet_history
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

SELECT e."Symptoms", p."Diagnosis" AS "Overall Diagnosis"
FROM main."Examination" AS e
JOIN main."Patient" AS p ON p."ID" = e."ID"
WHERE INSTR(',' || REPLACE(REPLACE(e."Diagnosis", ' ', ''), '+', ',') || ',', ',SLE,') > 0
  AND NULLIF(TRIM(e."Symptoms"), '') IS NOT NULL
  AND p."Birthday" IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY p."Birthday" DESC, p."ID" ASC, e."Examination Date" DESC
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH exams AS (
    SELECT e.ID, e."Examination Date", e.Symptoms,
           REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(TRIM(e.Diagnosis), '->', ','), '+', ','), ', ', ',') AS diseases
    FROM Examination AS e
    WHERE TRIM(e.Symptoms) <> ''
)
SELECT e.Symptoms, p.Diagnosis AS overall_diagnosis
FROM exams AS e
JOIN Patient AS p ON p.ID = e.ID
WHERE (',' || e.diseases || ',') LIKE '%,SLE,%'
  AND p.Birthday IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY p.Birthday DESC, p.ID ASC, e."Examination Date" DESC
LIMIT 1

Final SQL / answer

WITH bond_types(bond_type, sort_order) AS (
    VALUES ('-', 1), ('=', 2), ('#', 3)
),
carbon_carbon_bonds AS (
    SELECT b.bond_id, b.bond_type
    FROM main.bond AS b
    JOIN main.molecule AS m ON m.molecule_id = b.molecule_id
    JOIN main.connected AS c ON c.bond_id = b.bond_id
    JOIN main.atom AS a1 ON a1.atom_id = c.atom_id
    JOIN main.atom AS a2 ON a2.atom_id = c.atom_id2
    WHERE m.label = '+'
      AND a1.element = 'c'
      AND a2.element = 'c'
)
SELECT t.bond_type, COUNT(DISTINCT b.bond_id) AS bond_count
FROM bond_types AS t
LEFT JOIN carbon_carbon_bonds AS b ON b.bond_type = t.bond_type
GROUP BY t.bond_type, t.sort_order
ORDER BY t.sort_order
LIMIT 1000

WITH requested_types(bond_type, sort_order) AS (
    VALUES ('-', 1), ('=', 2), ('#', 3)
),
carbon_bonds AS (
    SELECT b.bond_type, b.bond_id
    FROM main.bond AS b
    JOIN main.molecule AS m ON m.molecule_id = b.molecule_id
    JOIN main.connected AS c ON c.bond_id = b.bond_id
    JOIN main.atom AS a1 ON a1.atom_id = c.atom_id
    JOIN main.atom AS a2 ON a2.atom_id = c.atom_id2
    WHERE m.label = '+'
      AND a1.element = 'c'
      AND a2.element = 'c'
)
SELECT t.bond_type, COUNT(DISTINCT b.bond_id) AS bond_count
FROM requested_types AS t
LEFT JOIN carbon_bonds AS b ON b.bond_type = t.bond_type
GROUP BY t.sort_order, t.bond_type
ORDER BY t.sort_order
LIMIT 3
